*Simon Althaus,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Max Mühlhäuser,Christian Reuter,Ephraim Zimmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WOOTdroid 是一个无需修改操作系统或应用插桩的 Android 设备端追踪方案。它通过 eBPF 实现高性能系统调用审计 (WDSys) 并在内核中捕获 Binder parcels 后解码 (WDBind)，解决了系统审计中的事件丢失和语义鸿沟两大问题。实验表明，WDSys 开销不超过 3.6%，比 ftrace 多追踪 33% 的系统调用；端到端案例成功重建了 10 个安全相关的 Binder 事务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android 系统审计面临两个主要问题：一是现有系统调用追踪器在负载下会丢失事件（用户空间读取速度跟不上内核缓冲区覆盖速度）；二是通过 Binder IPC 机制传递的安全相关应用行为对系统调用层不可见，存在语义鸿沟。现有方案要么修改 Android 平台（难以适配系统更新），要么在用户空间插桩（易被高级攻击者绕过）.

Method: WOOTdroid 包含两个核心组件：WDSys（基于 eBPF 移植 eAudit 风格的系统调用审计，运行于当前 Android 系统）和 WDBind（在内核中捕获 Binder parcels，并在进程外通过 Java 反射提取的框架签名表进行解码）。

Result: WDSys 在 Pixel 9 (Android 16) 上 Geekbench 开销不超过 3.6%，比 ftrace 多追踪 33% 的系统调用。端到端案例研究成功重建了 10 个安全相关的 Binder 事务。

Conclusion: WOOTdroid 在不修改操作系统或应用插桩的前提下，有效解决了 Android 系统审计中的事件丢失和语义鸿沟问题，为 stock Android 设备提供了一种高效、安全的追踪方案。

Abstract: System auditing on Android faces two problems. First, existing syscall tracers lose events under load, silently overwriting entries faster than a user space reader can drain them. Second, security-relevant application behavior is mediated through Binder, Android's kernel IPC mechanism, and is therefore hidden from the syscall layer. The Binder parcels that the kernel does see carry no method names or typed arguments, a disconnect between low-level events and high-level behavior known as the semantic gap. Existing approaches address the semantic gap either by modifying the Android platform, making them difficult to adjust to OS updates, or by instrumenting the traced application in user space, which sophisticated adversaries can evade by bypassing the instrumented framework APIs.
  We present WOOTdroid, a design and prototype for on-device tracing on stock Android that addresses both problems without OS modification or application instrumentation. WDSys, an eBPF port of eAudit-style syscall auditing, runs on current Android with at most 3.6% Geekbench overhead and traces 33% more syscalls than ftrace. WDBind captures Binder parcels in the kernel and decodes them out-of-process against a framework signature table extracted via Java reflection. We demonstrate WOOTdroid on Pixel 9 devices running Android 16 with an end-to-end case study reconstructing ten security-relevant Binder transactions.

</details>


### [121] [TwinGate: Stateful Defense against Decompositional Jailbreaks in Untraceable Traffic via Asymmetric Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27861)
*Bowen Sun,Chaozhuo Li,Yaodong Yang,Yiwei Wang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TwinGate 是一种面向分解式越狱攻击的状态化双编码器防御框架，通过非对称对比学习将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段聚类到共享潜在空间，在严格匿名化威胁模型下实现高召回率、低误报率及低延迟的实时防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略在真实部署中面临两大根本性局限：1）无法在缺乏可信用户元数据的情况下追踪全局历史上下文；2）使用生成模型进行实时监控带来计算开销过高的问题，因此需要一种轻量级、无需用户身份信息的防御方法。

Method: 提出 TwinGate 框架，包含两个核心组件：1）使用非对称对比学习（ACL）将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段在共享潜在空间中聚类；2）并行冻结编码器抑制良性主题重叠带来的误报。每个请求仅需一次轻量前向传播，可与目标模型的预填充阶段并行执行。

Result: 在包含超过362万条指令、覆盖8600个不同恶意意图的大规模数据集上，采用严格因果协议评估，TwinGate 实现了高恶意意图召回率和极低误报率，对自适应攻击具有强鲁棒性，并在吞吐量和延迟方面显著优于状态化与无状态基线方法。

Conclusion: TwinGate 有效解决了分解式越狱攻击在真实部署场景中的防御难题，通过轻量级状态化设计实现了高效、可扩展的实时防御，为LLM安全提供了重要保障。

Abstract: Decompositional jailbreaks pose a critical threat to large language models (LLMs) by allowing adversaries to fragment a malicious objective into a sequence of individually benign queries that collectively reconstruct prohibited content. In real-world deployments, LLMs face a continuous, untraceable stream of fully anonymized and arbitrarily interleaved requests, infiltrated by covertly distributed adversarial queries. Under this rigorous threat model, state-of-the-art defensive strategies exhibit fundamental limitations. In the absence of trustworthy user metadata, they are incapable of tracking global historical contexts, while their deployment of generative models for real-time monitoring introduces computationally prohibitive overhead. To address this, we present TwinGate, a stateful dual-encoder defense framework. TwinGate employs Asymmetric Contrastive Learning (ACL) to cluster semantically disparate but intent-matched malicious fragments in a shared latent space, while a parallel frozen encoder suppresses false positives arising from benign topical overlap. Each request requires only a single lightweight forward pass, enabling the defense to execute in parallel with the target model's prefill phase at negligible latency overhead. To evaluate our approach and advance future research, we construct a comprehensive dataset of over 3.62 million instructions spanning 8,600 distinct malicious intents. Evaluated on this large-scale corpus under a strictly causal protocol, TwinGate achieves high malicious intent recall at a remarkably low false positive rate while remaining highly robust against adaptive attacks. Furthermore, our proposal substantially outperforms stateful and stateless baselines, delivering superior throughput and reduced latency.

</details>


### [122] [Latent Adversarial Detection: Adaptive Probing of LLM Activations for Multi-Turn Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28129)
*Prashant Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"对抗性躁动"概念，发现多轮提示注入攻击在模型残差流中留下激活级轨迹信号，通过五个标量轨迹特征将对话级检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%，并在多模型家族和数据集上验证了其泛化性与数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本级防御难以检测看似良性的单轮对话中隐藏的多轮提示注入攻击，因此需要在激活层面寻找更可靠的检测信号。

Method: 分析多轮提示注入攻击在模型残差流中的激活轨迹，提取五个标量轨迹特征（总路径长度等）进行对话级检测；利用合成数据集的三阶段（良性/试探/对抗）逐轮标注训练检测器。

Result: 检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%；信号在4个模型家族（24B-70B）上可复现；三源联合训练在混合集上达到89.4%检测率、2.4%误报率；二元标注导致50-59%误报率。

Conclusion: 对抗性躁动是可靠的激活级检测信号，但检测器具有模型特异性且泛化依赖于数据分布覆盖，三阶段逐轮标注数据对实际部署至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-turn prompt injection follows a known attack path -- trust-building, pivoting, escalation but text-level defenses miss covert attacks where individual turns appear benign. We show this attack path leaves an activation-level signature in the model's residual stream: each phase shift moves the activation, producing a total path length far exceeding benign conversations. We call this adversarial restlessness. Five scalar trajectory features capturing this signal lift conversation-level detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic held-out data. The signal replicates across four model families (24B-70B); probes are model-specific and do not transfer across architectures. Generalization is source-dependent: leave-one-source-out evaluation shows each of synthetic, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and SafeDialBench captures distinct attack distributions, with detection on real-world LMSYS reaching 47-71% when its distribution is represented in training. Combined three-source training achieves 89.4% detection at 2.4% false positive rate on a held-out mixed set. We further show that three-phase turn-level labels(benign/pivoting/adversarial) unique to our synthetic dataset are essential: binary conversation-level labels produce 50-59% false positives. These results establish adversarial restlessness as a reliable activation-level signal and characterize the data requirements for practical deployment.

</details>


### [123] [FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28157)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlashRT是一个针对长上下文大语言模型的优化型红队测试框架，能够在提示注入和知识腐败攻击中实现2-7倍加速和2-4倍GPU内存节省，为系统化安全评估提供高效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在真实应用中广泛部署，但面临提示注入和知识腐败等安全威胁。现有优化型红队测试方法虽然攻击力强，但资源消耗巨大（计算和GPU内存），尤其对于长上下文场景，严重阻碍了社区（特别是学术界）对LLM安全风险的系统性评估和防御策略的有效性验证。

Method: 提出FlashRT框架，针对优化型提示注入和知识腐败攻击进行计算和内存效率优化，可广泛应用于TAP、AutoDAN等黑盒优化方法。

Result: 相比基准方法nanoGCG，FlashRT实现2-7倍速度提升（如从1小时降至10分钟内），2-4倍GPU内存减少（如32K token上下文从264.1 GB降至65.7 GB）。

Conclusion: FlashRT可作为高效的红队测试工具，支持长上下文LLM安全性的系统性评估，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Quantifying the Cost of Manual Navigation: A Comparison of Gesture-Based Magnification versus Direct Access Reading in Digital Layout-based Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27010)
*Sebastián Gallardo,Hui-Yin Wu,Dorian Mazauric,Pierre Kornprobst,Monica Di Meo,Stéphanie Baillif,Aurelie Calabrese*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 大字体排版相比手势缩放（捏合与平移）使数字报纸阅读速度提升18%、目标定位速度提升30%，且能恢复被手势交互破坏的自然阅读策略，同时降低用户工作负荷并提升偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化手动导航（手势缩放）与直接结构访问（大字体排版）在布局式数字文档中带来的行为与性能代价，探究视觉受限条件下结构线索（标题作为入口点）对阅读体验的影响。

Method: 受试者完成两项任务（朗读所有标题、定位目标文章），在两种条件下（原始版+手势缩放 vs 大字体直接访问版）进行实验，采集性能指标（成功率、完成时间）、阅读路径分析的行为完整性、以及NASA-TLX感知工作负荷与偏好。

Result: 大字体条件在阅读速度（提升18%）和目标定位速度（提升30%）上均优于手势缩放，且恢复了被手势交互破坏的自然阅读策略；受试者报告更低工作负荷和更高偏好。

Conclusion: 应开发自动生成大字体排版的方法，通过布局适配与字体缩放相结合来支持可访问性和体验质量。

Abstract: Understanding how diverse audiences engage with structured media is critical to ensure a consistent quality of experience. In this context, we quantify the behavioral and performance cost of manual navigation (e.g., pinch and zoom) versus direct structural access in layout-based digital documents. We specifically investigate newspaper reading when visual access to structural cues (headlines as entry points) is constrained. Participants completed two tasks-reading all headlines aloud and locating target articles-under two conditions: (1) original edition with gesture-based magnification (pan and zoom), which is the industry standard for digital documents, and (2) large-print edition supporting direct-access reading. We collected performance measures (success ratio and completion time), behavioral integrity through reading path analysis, alongside perceived workload and preferences (NASA-TLX). Results from linear mixed-effects models show that the large-print condition yielded not only better performance than gesture-based magnification (18% improvement in reading speed, 30% improvement in speed to locate a target), but more importantly, restored the natural reading strategy that gesture-based magnification interaction disrupts. Readers also reported lower workload and higher preference. These findings highlight the importance of developing automated methods for generating large-print editions, where layout adaptation complements font scaling to support accessibility and quality of experience.

</details>


### [125] [Breaking Bad Financial Habits: How LLM Conversations Correct Financial Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27022)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过三项预注册研究，发现大语言模型（LLM）能够持久纠正金融误解，但需要两个关键条件：纠正意图（而非无方向对话）和接收者接受度（回应需匹配用户的金融素养水平）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融误解会带来直接经济成本（如恐慌性抛售、回避股票市场），且传统金融素养干预成本高、覆盖有限、知识与行为转化之间存在顽固缺口，亟需可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 实施三项预注册研究，比较不同LLM提示策略（有纠正意图vs.无方向讨论vs.无辅助自我反思）对金融误解纠正效果的影响，并考察LLM回应与参与者金融素养的匹配程度对纠正效果的作用。

Result: 仅有纠正意图的LLM讨论产生的纠正效果并不优于无辅助自我反思；无方向的LLM对话甚至会加深误解；LLM回应低于参与者金融素养水平时被认为可信度较低，纠正效果也显著减弱。

Conclusion: LLM可作为传统金融素养干预的可扩展替代方案，但必须同时具备纠正意图和受众接受度两个设计要素才能有效纠正金融误解。

Abstract: Financial misconceptions carry direct economic costs, from panic selling to equity market avoidance, yet they are notoriously resistant to correction. Traditional financial literacy interventions are constrained by cost, reach, and a persistent gap between knowledge and behavioral change. Across three pre-registered studies, we find that purposefully designed LLMs can durably correct financial misconceptions. Critically, two factors are necessary for this effect. First, corrective intent: LLMs prompted only to discuss a misconception produce corrections no better than unassisted self-reflection, and undirected LLM conversations can actively entrench misconceptions. Second, recipient receptivity: financial concepts are often foreign to the investors who misapply them, and LLM responses pitched below a participant's financial sophistication are judged as less credible and produce substantially weaker corrections. LLMs thus offer a scalable alternative to traditional financial literacy intervention, but only when designed with both factors in mind.

</details>


### [126] [What Influences Readers' and Writers' Perceived Necessity of AI Disclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27129)
*Jingchao Fang,Victoria Xiaohan Wen,Mina Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过一项727人的小场景研究，发现AI写作披露的必要性受视角（读者vs作者）、目的及过程因素（可替代性、直接性、意图性）影响，努力程度无显著作用，且作者意图对读者和作者产生相反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究哪些因素影响人们对AI写作中披露使用情况的必要性感知，从视角、目的和过程因素三个维度展开。

Method: Vignette研究（小场景实验），N=727，操纵视角（读者/作者）、目的以及AI使用的过程因素（可替代性、努力程度、意图性、直接性）。

Result: 读者比作者认为更需披露；AI贡献不可替代、直接融入、无作者意图时披露必要性更高；作者意图对读者和作者产生相反影响；努力程度无显著效应。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了读者与作者对AI披露的朴素判断差异，为现有AI使用透明度规范提供反思视角，并为设计更符合双方感知的披露指南和工具提供启示。

Abstract: The growing capability of artificial intelligence (AI) leads to its increasing adoption in writing, spurring discussions around whether writers should disclose their AI use in writing. What influences the perceived necessity of disclosure? We look into this question from three dimensions: perspective (reader or writer of the text), purpose (the goal of reading or writing), and procedural factors (how AI was used in the writing process in terms of replaceability, effortfulness, intentionality, and directness). In a vignette study (N = 727), we find that readers consider disclosure to be more necessary than writers, and disclosure is regarded as more necessary when AI's contribution in writing is irreplaceable, directly incorporated, and when the writer does not intentionally steer AI generation. To our surprise, the writers' intentionality of AI use produces contrasting effects on readers' and writers' perceived necessity of disclosure. Moreover, the effort of writing shows no significant effect on the perceived necessity. This study contributes to the conversation on transparent AI use by revealing readers' and writers' grassroots judgments, providing a unique angle to reflect on existing regulations, and offering insights into how AI disclosure guidance and tools could be designed to better align with readers' and writers' perceptions.

</details>


### [127] [Reading Speed, Image Quality Ratings, and Comfort Ratings in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27203)
*Minjung Kim,Saeideh Ghahghaei Nezamabadi,Trisha Lian,Anand Singh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Read-AR是一个增强现实中阅读的基准数据集，包含超过11,000个阅读速度和近6,000个视觉质量与舒适度评分，涵盖80多种实验条件，旨在为不同AR头显架构提供质量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本渲染和显示是增强现实（AR）的关键应用场景，但目前缺乏一个统一的、受控条件下的基准数据集来评估不同AR头显架构在阅读任务上的表现质量。

Method: 在相同的实验装置上，收集了超过80种不同实验条件下的11,000多个阅读速度数据和近6,000个视觉质量与舒适度评分，构建了Read-AR数据集。

Result: 构建了一个包含大规模阅读速度和主观评分的数据集，覆盖多种实验条件，且实验条件一致可控。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为不同AR头显架构阅读质量的参考基准，有助于推动AR文本显示质量的标准化评估。

Abstract: The rendering and display of text is a key use-case for augmented reality (AR). Here, we present the Read-AR, a dataset of reading in AR, for which we collected over 11,000 reading speeds and almost 6000 visual quality and comfort ratings across over 80 different experiment conditions on the same experiment set-up. The consistent, controlled set-up enables the dataset to function as a reference for benchmarking the quality of different AR headset architectures.

</details>


### [128] [Upskilling with Generative AI: Practices and Challenges for Freelance Knowledge Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27231)
*Kashif Imteyaz,Isabel Lopez,Nakul Rajpal,Hunjun Shin,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对自由职业者使用生成式AI工具进行自我导向学习的研究，发现AI被用于辅助学习结构化和探索性技能获取，但并非主要学习资源；揭示了从"成长型学习"向"生存型学习"的转变，以及"隐性能力"这一结构性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由职业者缺乏传统雇员的培训、指导和基础设施支持，需不断获取新技能以保持竞争力；生成式AI工具既改变了市场需求，也提供了新的学习支持方式，但对其实际使用情况缺乏了解。

Method: 混合方法研究：结合问卷调查和半结构化访谈，以自我导向学习理论为框架，研究自由职业知识工作者如何使用生成式AI工具进行学习。

Result: 自由职业者越来越依赖生成式AI来构建学习结构和支持探索性技能获取；但由于AI输出不一致、缺乏情境相关性及需要验证开销，并未将其作为主要学习资源；出现从"成长型学习"向"生存型学习"的转变；识别出"隐性能力"问题——通过AI获得技能但缺乏可信的验证和信号传递方式。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，为面向自由职业者的生成式AI赋能学习工具提出了设计建议。

Abstract: Freelance workers must continually acquire new skills to remain competitive in online labor markets, yet they lack the organizational training, mentorship, and infrastructure available to traditional employees. Generative AI-powered tools like ChatGPT are reshaping market skill demands while also offering new forms of on-demand learning support to meet those demands. Despite growing interest in AI-powered learning tools, little is known about how freelancers actually use these tools to learn, the challenges they encounter, and how generative AI for learning interacts with precarity and competition in platform-based work. We present a mixed-methods study combining a survey and semi-structured interviews with freelance knowledge workers. Grounded in self-directed learning theory, we examine how freelancers integrate generative AI tools into their learning practices. Our findings show that freelancers increasingly rely on generative AI to structure learning and support exploratory skill acquisition, but do not treat it as their primary learning resource due to inconsistency, lack of contextual relevance, and verification overhead. We identify a shift from learning as growth to learning as survival, where upskilling is oriented toward immediate market viability rather than long-term development. We also surface a structural challenge we term invisible competencies, in which workers acquire skills through generative AI tools but lack credible ways to signal or validate these skills in competitive freelance markets. Based on these insights, we offer design recommendations for generative AI-powered learning tools for freelancers.

</details>


### [129] [Evaluating Epistemic Guardrails in AI Reading Assistants: A Behavioral Audit of a Minimal Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27275)
*Matthew Christian Agustin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出“认知护栏”概念，通过TextWalk阅读辅助原型在12篇分析性文本上应用十轮提示协议，研究了LLM阅读助手在解释性任务中出现的“解释性置换”问题，发现系统最关键的弱点并非完全崩溃，而是在支持与替代之间的中间地带——系统保持合理但过度转移了解释性劳动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM阅读助手越来越多地用于需要解释而非简单检索的场景，其核心风险不仅是错误或不安全输出，更是“解释性置换”——意义建构工作从读者向系统的转移。现有研究缺乏对AI系统如何参与阅读与解释过程的约束机制（即认知护栏）的系统性考察。

Method: 使用最小化阅读支持原型TextWalk（设计为共同阅读者而非答案提供者），对12篇涵盖四类论证性散文的分析性文本应用固定十轮提示协议。协议从基线阅读支持逐步升级到解释性探究、边界压力测试和显性捷径压力，将护栏视为交互中可观察的行为属性而非静态指令特征来检验。

Result: 强基线稳定性；解释性探究阶段出现可测量的应变；直接边界压力下部分恢复；升级压力下后期稳定。最关键的弱点表现为系统保持合理且具有教学性，但过度转移了解释性劳动——处于支持与替代之间的中间地带。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：评估认知护栏作为交互现象的协议；认知护栏在压力下行为动态的经验性描述；以及阅读支持AI中解释边界功能的初步模型。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) reading assistants are increasingly used in settings that require interpretation rather than simple retrieval. In these contexts, the central risk is not only error or unsafe output, but interpretive displacement: the transfer of meaning-making work from reader to system. This paper examines that problem through the concept of epistemic guardrails, defined here as constraints on how an artificial intelligence (AI) system participates in reading and interpretation. Using TextWalk, a minimal reading-support prototype designed as a co-reader rather than an answer-provider, the study applies a fixed ten-prompt protocol to twelve analytical texts spanning four categories of argumentative prose. The protocol escalates from baseline reading support to interpretive inquiry, boundary stress, and explicit shortcut pressure, enabling guardrails to be examined as behavioral properties observable in interaction rather than as static instruction features. Results show strong baseline stability, measurable strain during interpretive inquiry, partial recovery under direct boundary stress, and late-stage stabilization under escalation pressure. The most consequential weaknesses did not appear as overt collapse, but in a middle zone between support and substitution, where the system remained grounded and pedagogical while redistributing too much interpretive labor away from the reader. The paper contributes a protocol for evaluating epistemic guardrails as interactional phenomena in conversational AI reading assistants, an empirical account of their behavioral dynamics under pressure, and an emerging model of interpretive boundary function in reading-support AI.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All Exercises: Personalizing Computer Science Worksheets with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27433)
*Franco Ortiz,Runlong Ye,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出FACET系统，利用LLM根据学习者画像（基于布鲁姆分类法和自我决定理论）为CS1课程生成个性化正则表达式练习材料。实验表明，个性化材料使低知识学生的学习完成率从70-75%提升至99%以上，正确率提高18.2%，而高表现学生出现天花板效应。学生更重视结构性支架而非动机性语气。LLM个性化主要作为保留支架，防止高风险学生放弃任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在教育中的应用主要面向学生，本研究探索将LLM用于支持教师，通过FACET系统自动生成个性化教学内容，以弥补传统一刀切教学在CS1课程中导致的学生参与度差异和任务放弃问题。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，对409名大一CS学生进行正则表达式知识评估和动机评估，将学生分为四种学习者画像（高知识/高动机、高知识/低动机、低知识/高动机、低知识/低动机），治疗组接受LLM生成的个性化练习（基于布鲁姆分类法和自我决定理论调整支架、教学明确性和语气），对照组接受标准非自适应练习。

Result: 标准练习中低知识学生约25-30%任务未完成，个性化材料下各画像完成率均超过99%；低知识/低动机学生在个性化支持下正确率提高18.2%；高表现学生出现天花板效应；调查显示学生更重视结构支架（逻辑顺序、难度节奏）而非动机语气；学生认为自适应任务与标准任务难度相当。

Conclusion: 基于学习者画像的LLM个性化主要作为保留支架，防止高风险学生放弃任务，同时不削弱任务的"理想难度"。面向教师的LLM系统可通过调整教学明确性有效缩小CS1课程的参与度差距。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to student-facing educational tools, this work explores their use in supporting instructors by presenting a practical adaptation of the Framework for Adaptive Content using Educational Technology (FACET) system to generate personalized instructional materials for an Introduction to Computer Programming (CS1) course.
  We conducted a mixed-methods study with 409 first-year computer science (CS) students, focusing on regular expressions (RegEx). Students were assessed on their knowledge and motivation, classified into one of four learner profiles, and assigned either LLM-personalized (treatment) or standard non-adaptive (control) exercises. Personalized materials varied in scaffolding, instructional explicitness, and tone based on learner profiles grounded in Bloom's Taxonomy and Self-Determination Theory.
  Quantitative analysis reveals that standard exercises resulted in task incompletion among low-knowledge learners, with approximately 25-30% incompletion, whereas personalized materials sustained near-universal completion (>99%) across all profiles. While high-performing students experienced ceiling effects, Low Knowledge/Low Motivation students achieved significantly higher correctness (+18.2%) with personalized support. Survey data indicate that students prioritize structural scaffolding (logical sequence, difficulty pacing) over motivational tone and perceive the adaptive tasks as equally challenging as standard exercises.
  These findings suggest that learner-profile-driven LLM personalization primarily serves as a retention scaffold, preventing task abandonment among at-risk students without diminishing the task's "desirable difficulty". The results demonstrate that instructor-facing LLM systems can effectively close engagement gaps in CS1 by tailoring instructional explicitness to student needs.

</details>


### [131] [Why Learners Drift In and Out: Examining Intermittent Discontinuance in AI-Mediated Informal Digital English Learning (AI-IDLE) Using SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27493)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架，通过定量（SEM）和定性比较分析（fsQCA）方法，探讨了中国大学生在AI辅助非正式英语学习中的间歇性中断行为。结果表明，感知智能、互动性和个性化通过愉悦感间接减少中断，而感知无效、不可控和复杂性通过厌倦感间接增加中断。fsQCA识别出四种导致中断的组态路径，揭示了认知障碍与情感脱离的不同组合均可导致学习者暂时退出AI-IDLE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI语言学习研究多聚焦于初次采纳和持续使用，忽略了用户在使用过程中的间歇性中断行为。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究AI-IDLE情境下导致学习者暂时退出而非永久放弃的认知与情感机制。

Method: 研究采用问卷调查法，收集632名具有AI-IDLE经验的中国大学英语学习者的数据，通过结构方程模型（SEM）检验变量间的间接效应，并运用模糊集定性比较分析（fsQCA）识别导致间歇性中断的组态路径。

Result: SEM结果显示：感知智能、感知互动性和感知个性化通过愉悦感间接降低中断行为；感知无效、感知不可控性和感知复杂性通过厌倦感间接增加中断行为。fsQCA识别出四种等价的组态路径，表明认知障碍与情感脱离的不同组合均可导致间歇性中断。

Conclusion: 研究将AI-IDLE研究从采纳和持续使用拓展到采纳后中断阶段，强调在AI支持的语言学习体验设计中，应注重愉悦性、个性化、可控性和认知可管理性，以降低学习者的间歇性中断行为。

Abstract: This study examined intermittent discontinuance in AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) through the cognition-affect-conation framework. Survey data were collected from 632 Chinese university EFL learners with prior AI-IDLE experience and analysed using structural equation modelling and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis. The SEM results showed that perceived intelligence, perceived interactivity, and perceived personalisation reduced AI-IDLE intermittent discontinuance indirectly through enjoyment, whereas perceived ineffectiveness, perceived uncontrollability, and perceived complexity increased discontinuance indirectly through boredom. The fsQCA results further identified four configurational pathways leading to intermittent discontinuance, indicating that learners' temporary withdrawal from AI-IDLE can result from different combinations of cognitive barriers and affective disengagement. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and continuance to post-adoption discontinuance and highlight the need to design AI-supported English learning experiences that are enjoyable, personalised, controllable, and cognitively manageable.

</details>


### [132] [From Elastic to Viscoelastic: An EEMD-Enhanced Pulse Transit Time Model for Robust Blood Pressure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27500)
*Boyuan Gu,Yijin Yang,Shuaiqi Cheng,Xiaorong Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息框架的无袖带血压估计方法，通过引入粘弹性补偿机制（利用EEMD分解定义粘弹性速度指标），在MIMIC-II数据库364名受试者（28,525个心动周期）上实现收缩压RMSE 5.22 mmHg、舒张压RMSE 3.65 mmHg，R>0.97的医学级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Moens-Korteweg方程的PTT血压估计模型假设动脉壁为纯弹性体，忽略了血管固有粘弹性，导致在血液动力学快速波动时估计精度下降。

Method: 1) 使用Modified Akima插值对原始PPG信号进行高保真重构；2) 采用鲁棒的相交切线法精确定位脉搏波脚点；3) 利用集合经验模态分解(EEMD)分离高频本征模函数(IMFs)，定义"粘弹性速度指标"来量化血管阻尼效应（η·έ）。

Result: 在MIMIC-II数据库（364名受试者，28,525个心动周期，高血压患病率23.4%）上验证，收缩压RMSE为5.22 mmHg，舒张压RMSE为3.65 mmHg，Pearson相关系数R>0.97。

Conclusion: 融入粘弹性特征显著增强了对血管滞后的鲁棒性，提升了无袖带血压估计在动态条件下的准确性。

Abstract: Cuffless blood pressure (BP) estimation based on Pulse Transit Time (PTT) has emerged as a promising solution for continuous health monitoring. However, conventional models relying on the Moens-Korteweg equation often fail during rapid hemodynamic fluctuations, as they assume arterial walls are purely elastic and neglect inherent viscoelasticity. To address this limitation, we propose a physics-informed framework introducing a viscoelastic compensation mechanism. First, raw photoplethysmogram (PPG) signals undergo high-fidelity reconstruction using Modified Akima (Makima) interpolation. Second, a robust Intersecting Tangent Method is applied for precise pulse foot localization. Crucially, we utilize Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD) to isolate high-frequency Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), defining a ``Viscoelastic Velocity Metric'' to quantify the vascular damping effect ($η\cdot \dotε$) typically ignored by elastic models. The framework was rigorously validated on a challenging subset of the MIMIC-II database (364 subjects, 28,525 cardiac cycles) characterized by a high prevalence of hypertension (23.4\%). Experimental results demonstrate medical-grade accuracy, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 5.22 mmHg for Systolic and 3.65 mmHg for Diastolic BP, with Pearson correlation coefficients ($R > 0.97$). These findings confirm that incorporating viscoelastic features significantly enhances robustness against vascular hysteresis.

</details>


### [133] [Examining discontinuance of AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) among university students: Evidence from SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27506)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架，调查了中国大学生在AI辅助非正式英语学习中停止使用意图的影响因素。SEM分析表明，不满和挫折感正向预测停止使用意图（挫折感影响更强）；失验、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响两种情感反应。fsQCA进一步揭示了导致高停止使用意图的多种条件配置，表明停止使用行为受因果复杂性和等效性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI-IDLE研究多关注采用和持续使用阶段，鲜有探讨学习者在AI辅助非正式英语学习中的停止使用（discontinuance）行为及其心理机制。本研究旨在填补这一空白，从负面视角理解AI语言学习工具的退出现象。

Method: 采用横截面问卷设计，收集746名有AI工具使用经验的中国大学生数据。结合结构方程模型（SEM）进行路径关系检验，同时使用模糊集定性比较分析（fsQCA）探索导致高停止使用意图的充分条件配置。

Result: SEM结果显示：不满和挫折感均正向预测停止使用意图，挫折感的预测效应更强；失验、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响不满与挫折感。fsQCA结果识别出多种足以导致高停止使用意图的条件组合，表明停止使用行为由复杂因果路径和等效性决定，而非单一必要条件。

Conclusion: 本研究将AI-IDLE研究从"采纳-参与"阶段拓展至"采纳后脱离"阶段。实践上，需减少学习者的不满、挫折感、感知复杂性和感知风险，以降低AI辅助非正式英语学习中的用户流失。

Abstract: This study examined university students' discontinuance intention towards AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE). Drawing on the cognition-affect-conation framework, the study investigated how three cognitive factors, namely disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk, influence two affective responses, namely dissatisfaction and frustration, and how these affective responses predict discontinuance intention. A cross-sectional survey was conducted with 746 Chinese university students who had experience using AI tools for informal English learning. Data were analysed using structural equation modelling (SEM) and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis (fsQCA). The SEM results showed that dissatisfaction and frustration positively predicted discontinuance intention, with frustration showing the stronger effect. Disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk also positively influenced dissatisfaction and frustration. The fsQCA results further identified multiple sufficient configurations leading to high AI-IDLE discontinuance intention, indicating that discontinuance is shaped by causal complexity and equifinality rather than by a single necessary condition. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and engagement to post-adoption disengagement and provide implications for reducing learners' dissatisfaction, frustration, perceived complexity, and risk in AI-supported informal English learning.

</details>


### [134] [I'm Fine, But My Voice Isn't: Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection for Reflective Journaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27517)
*Sumin Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模态情感失调检测（CADD）任务，用于检测数字日记中文本与语音情感的不一致（掩盖、应对、一致三类）；构建了CADD-Journal数据集和DACM双编码器非对称交叉注意力模型（宏F1=0.711），并通过零样本评估揭示了TTS训练模型与真实语音之间的领域差距，最后开发了ReflectJournal iOS应用进行实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字日记存在"真实性鸿沟"：用户即使在私密写作中也会有意识地修饰情感表达，将原始情绪转化为经过筛选的文字。这种文本与真实情感之间的失配亟需形式化检测方法。

Method: (1) 形式化定义CADD为三分类任务（掩盖、应对、一致），基于Gross情绪调节过程模型；(2) 构建CADD-Journal数据集（1,800样本），采用共享句子池设计隔离声学与文本信号；(3) 提出DACM双编码器模型，使用非对称交叉注意力解决池化融合中的梯度退化问题；(4) 进行四步消融实验分析各模块贡献；(5) 在三个自然语料库上进行零样本评估量化领域差距；(6) 开发ReflectJournal iOS应用。

Result: DACM模型宏F1达到0.711；消融实验表明非对称注意力是主要驱动因素（提升+0.242），而DIM仅在跨模态特征上有效（+0.033）；零样本评估揭示TTS训练模型与真实语音之间存在显著领域差距。

Conclusion: 非对称交叉注意力是跨模态情感失调检测的关键技术；现有TTS数据训练的模型在真实场景中存在明显领域差距，需要针对自然语料库构建的两项具体建议；ReflectJournal为未来自然场景数据收集提供了部署平台。

Abstract: Digital journaling creates an authenticity gap: users consciously translate raw emotions into text, often sanitizing narratives even in private writing. We formalize this as Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection (CADD), a directional three-way classification distinguishing Masking (positive text, negative acoustics), Coping (negative text, positive acoustics), and Congruent utterances, grounded in Gross's process model of emotion regulation. We present three further contributions: (i) CADD-Journal, a 1,800-sample TTS dataset with a shared-sentence-pool design that provably isolates acoustic signal from textual content; (ii) DACM, a dual-encoder model with asymmetric cross-modal attention that re-solves a gradient degeneracy in pooled fusion, achieving macro-F1 0.711 - with a four-step ablation demonstrating that asymmetric attention is the dominant driver (+ 0.242) while the DIM is effective only on cross-modal features (+0.033); and (iii) a domain gap quantification: zero-shot evaluation across three naturalistic corpora reveals a substantial gap between TTS-trained models and real speech, and we identify two concrete requirements for future in-the-wild corpus construction. ReflectJournal, a proof-of-concept iOS application, operationalizes the framework and provides a deployment platform for naturalistic data collection.

</details>


### [135] [lpviz: Interactive Linear Programming Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27518)
*Evan Grand,Michael Klamkin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: lpviz 是一个基于浏览器的交互式线性规划可视化工具，支持用户直接绘制和编辑可行域与目标向量，对比 Simplex、Interior-Point、Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient、Central Path 等多种算法，提供 3D 模式展示迭代元数据（如互补间隙、KKT 残差），已用于科研和教学，开源免费发布于 https://lpviz.net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性规划工具往往需要用户手动操作原始数值系数，缺乏直观的交互界面，难以帮助用户直观理解不同算法的行为差异和收敛特性。

Method: 开发了一个基于浏览器的交互式可视化工具 lpviz，允许用户通过直接绘制和编辑的方式操纵可行域与目标向量，支持多种 LP 算法（Simplex、Interior-Point、Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient、Central Path）的对比，并在 3D 模式下将迭代点的高度映射到求解器元数据（如互补间隙、KKT 残差）。

Result: lpviz 已在科研和课堂教学中得到应用，帮助用户建立对不同求解器优劣以及求解器设置对收敛行为影响的直觉理解。

Conclusion: lpviz 作为开源免费的工具，为线性规划算法教学与研究提供了一种直观、交互式的可视化方案。

Abstract: This paper presents lpviz, a browser-based visualization tool for linear programming. lpviz is deeply interactive, offering an intuitive interface where users can directly draw and edit the feasible region and objective vector, without requiring cumbersome manipulation of raw numerical coefficients. lpviz lets users compare the behavior of several classes of linear programming algorithms, namely Simplex, Interior-Point, Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient, and Central Path. In the 3D mode, lpviz places iterates at heights corresponding to important solver metadata such as complementarity gap or KKT residual, helping users gain further insight into algorithm behavior beyond the primal iterates alone. lpviz has been used in both research and classroom settings, to help develop intuition for the strengths and weaknesses of different solvers and the impact of solver settings on convergence behavior. lpviz is open-source, permissively licensed, and freely available on any device with a web browser at https://lpviz.net .

</details>


### [136] [Knowledge Affordances for Hybrid Human-AI Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27539)
*Irene Celino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出“知识可供性”（Knowledge Affordance, KA）概念，用于系统化描述在混合人机信息生态中，智能体如何识别有意义的信息寻求机会。KA是声明式的、基于语义的描述，阐明知识源能提供什么、适合何种问题及在何种情境下有效，具有关系性和情境依赖性。论文连接了可供性理论、语义Web服务、知识工程和互理解性等多个研究领域，并展望了构建KA感知系统的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日益异构的信息生态中，人类和人工智能体面临一个尚未解决的基本问题：当寻求知识时，应该问谁以及为什么。现有方法缺乏一种系统化的方式来描述知识源能为特定问题和情境提供什么。

Method: 提出知识可供性（KA）作为概念框架。KA被定义为一组声明式的、基于语义的描述，包含三个核心维度：知识源能提供什么、适合哪些类型的问题、以及具有哪些情境属性。KA被定性为关系性的，即从智能体的任务、偏好和情境因素的交互中涌现。

Result: 本文并非提出完整框架，而是提供一个概念性提案，连接了可供性理论、语义Web服务、知识工程与查询、以及互理解性等多个研究领域。

Conclusion: KA概念为构建更具透明度、适应性和共享理解的信息空间导航系统提供了理论基础，并指出了未来构建KA感知系统的研究方向。

Abstract: As information ecosystems grow more heterogeneous, both humans and artificial agents increasingly face a simple yet unresolved question: when seeking knowledge, whom should we ask, and why? Inspired by how people intuitively "read a room", this paper introduces the concept of knowledge affordance (KA) to systematize how agents identify meaningful opportunities for information seeking in hybrid human-AI environments.
  Rather than introducing a fully formed framework, we propose KAs as declarative, semantically grounded descriptions of what a knowledge source can offer, for which kinds of questions, and with which contextual properties. Additionally, we suggest that KAs are relational, possibly emerging from the interplay between the agent's task, preferences and situational factors.
  Our contribution is thus a conceptual proposal that connects different research streams, including affordances, semantic web services, knowledge engineering and querying, and mutual intelligibility. We sketch possible research directions to build KA-aware systems that navigate information spaces with greater transparency, adaptability and shared understanding.

</details>


### [137] [Users' Activity Logs: the Good, the Bad, the Misconception, and the Disastrous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27676)
*Eman Alashwali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对沙特阿拉伯30名Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据进行二次分析，从用户视角探讨了活动日志的积极、消极、灾难性方面以及误解，提供了对用户感知的平衡理解，并为服务提供商、隐私研究人员和用户提出了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于用户活动日志的负面方面（如用户缺乏隐私控制意识、对数据的隐私担忧），缺乏对用户感知的全面平衡视角。本研究旨在填补这一空白，同时考察活动日志的积极、消极和极端负面（灾难性）方面及用户的误解。

Method: 对30名沙特阿拉伯Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据进行二次分析，采用模板分析方法，从四个主题维度（好、坏、误解、灾难）对数据进行分析。

Result: 发现了新的主题和使用场景，揭示了用户对活动日志的平衡感知，包括积极用途、隐私担忧、误解以及灾难性后果的认知。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对用户活动日志感知的更全面理解，为后续研究提供了有价值的参考，并向服务提供商、隐私研究人员/专家以及用户分别提出了实践建议。

Abstract: Most service providers, such as Google, save logs from data generated by users while using the service. Many service providers provide users with privacy controls to manage whether, how, and for how long the data is saved and used by the service provider. While most prior studies focused on the negative side of users' activity logs, such as users' lack of awareness about the logs' privacy controls and users' privacy concerns toward their data, this work aims to provide a balanced view of users' perceptions regarding activity logs by considering the positive, negative, and extremely negative (hence disastrous) sides, as well as the misconceptions of activity logs. In this work, we present a case study of Google's Activity controls by conducting a secondary analysis of interview data from 30 Google personal account holders in Saudi Arabia. Using template analysis, we analyzed the data from the lens of four main themes: the good, the bad, the misconception, and the disastrous aspects of users' activity logs from the users' perspective. Our findings uncover new themes and use cases, offering a balanced view of users' perceptions of activity logs, and provide a better understanding and a useful source for subsequent studies on related topics. We conclude with practical recommendations for service providers, privacy researchers and experts, and users alike.

</details>


### [138] ["It depends on where AI is used": Players' attitude patterns and evaluative logics toward different AI applications in digital games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27812)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Jiangxu Lin,Wenran Chen,Fei Qin,Zheyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于310份问卷的1,856条有效开放回答，研究了玩家对游戏中八种AI应用场景（智能NPC、涌现叙事、动态平衡、推荐系统、审查治理、美术资产生成、协作创作、玩法进化）的接受模式。玩家在AI增强沉浸感、个性化、新颖性、效率时接受，在威胁创造性、情感真实性、自主性、公平性、系统稳定性、作者身份或问责性时拒绝。识别出六种评价逻辑：体验丰富、工具效率、系统可靠、能动与控制、作者与合规、人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在数字游戏中的深度嵌入，玩家的态度不仅取决于AI是否被使用，还取决于它在游戏中的介入方式和场景，因此需要系统性地理解玩家对不同AI应用语境的态度差异。

Method: 收集310份问卷中的1,856条有效开放回答，采用主题分析法识别玩家接受、拒绝和条件性接受的原因，并进一步归纳出六种评价逻辑。

Result: 玩家在AI增强沉浸感、个性化、新颖性、效率或便利性时持欢迎态度；在AI威胁创造性、情感真实性、自主性、公平性、系统稳定性、作者身份或问责性时持抵制态度。识别出六种评价逻辑：体验丰富、工具效率、系统可靠、能动与控制、作者与合规、人类监督。

Conclusion: 数字游戏中AI接受具有高度情境敏感性，玩家对AI的评价取决于具体应用场景及其对玩家核心关切的增益或威胁。

Abstract: As AI becomes increasingly embedded in digital games, players' attitudes de-pend not only on whether AI is used, but also on where and how it intervenes in gameplay. This study examines players' evaluative patterns toward eight AI application contexts, including intelligent NPCs, emergent narrative, dynamic balancing, recommendation systems, review and governance, art asset generation, co-creation gameplay, and gameplay evolution. Based on 1,856 valid open-ended responses from 310 questionnaires, we conducted thematic analysis to identify reasons for acceptance, rejection, and conditional acceptance. Results show that players welcomed AI when it enhanced immersion, personalization, novelty, efficiency, or convenience, but resisted it when it threatened creativity, emotional authenticity, autonomy, fairness, system stability, authorship, or accountability. We further identify six evaluative logics: experiential enrichment, instrumental efficiency, system reliability, agency and control, authorship and compliance, and human oversight. These preliminary findings highlight the context-sensitive nature of AI acceptance in digital games.

</details>


### [139] [CoNewsReader: Supporting Comprehensive Understanding and Raising Critical Thoughts on Social Media News Through Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27905)
*Kangyu Yuan,Guanzheng Chen,Sizhe Liang,Hehai Lin,Qingyu Guo,Dingdong Liu,Xiaojuan Ma,Zhenhui Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并开发了基于评论的大型语言模型驱动工具CoNewsReader，通过提供补充信息、过滤有用评论和生成批判性问题来帮助用户在社交媒体上进行批判性新闻阅读。用户实验表明，相比传统社交媒体阅读界面，CoNewsReader能提升用户的新闻理解、批判性思维和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批判性新闻阅读对普通人（尤其是从社交媒体获取信息的用户）具有挑战性，而新闻评论区包含丰富的信息和多元观点，但目前缺乏如何利用这些评论来支持用户进行批判性新闻阅读的研究。

Method: 首先通过文献调研和形成性研究（N=12）提炼用户需求，然后开发基于大语言模型的交互式评论辅助工具CoNewsReader，最后通过24名大学生的受试者内实验评估效果。

Result: 相比基线社交媒体新闻阅读界面，使用CoNewsReader的参与者在新闻理解和批判性思维方面表现更好，且拥有更投入的阅读体验。

Conclusion: 评论驱动的交互式工具能有效支持批判性新闻阅读，并提出了利用机器生成内容辅助阅读任务的设计建议。

Abstract: Critical news reading (CNR), which requires grasping the holistic ideas of and raising critical thoughts on the news, is beneficial yet challenging for general people who usually get information on daily social media. Comments under the news can aid CNR by providing complementary information and other readers' diverse and critical thoughts. However, it is under-investigated how to leverage these comments to support users in CNR. In this paper, we first derive user requirements for a comment-based CNR tool from literature and a formative study (N=12). Then, we develop CoNewsReader, a comment-based interactive CNR tool powered by a large language model. CoNewsReader supports users in grasping the news idea with complementary information from comments, filtering useful comments for CNR, and getting questions generated based on the comments to conduct critical thinking. Our within-subjects study with 24 university students indicates that compared to a baseline news reading interface in social media, participants with CoNewsReader have a more engaging CNR experience and perform better on comprehending the news and raising critical thoughts. We discuss design considerations for supporting reading tasks with user- and machine-generated content.

</details>


### [140] [Enhancing multimodal affect recognition in healthcare: the robustness of appraisal dimensions over labels within age groups and in cross-age generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27938)
*Hippolyte Fournier,Sina Alisamir,Safaa Azzakhnini,Isabella Zsoldos,Eléonore Trân,Gérard Bailly,Frédéric Elisei,Béatrice Bouchot,Brice Varini,Patrick Constant,Joan Fruitet,Franck Tarpin-Bernard,Solange Rossato,François Portet,Olivier Koenig,Hanna Chainay,Fabien Ringeval*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于评价理论维度与分类标签的多模态情感识别模型在不同年龄段（老年与青年）CCT数据集上的表现，发现评价维度在预测准确性和跨年龄泛化能力上显著优于分类标签，且跨年龄训练未能提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感识别在AI辅助医疗（特别是计算机化认知训练CCT）中仍是重大挑战，现有研究缺乏对不同年龄段情感识别模型泛化能力的系统比较，亟需验证基于评价理论维度的情感建模方法是否优于传统分类标签方法。

Method: 基于THERADIA-WoZ老年语料库，扩展收集青年数据集；构建多模态情感识别模型，比较评价理论维度与分类标签两种标注范式；采用语料内、跨语料、混合语料三种评估方案，系统比较模型的预测准确性和泛化能力。

Result: 评价维度在所有条件下均优于分类标签，预测精度更高且更稳定；分类标签在跨年龄评估中性能降至随机水平，而评价维度仍保持显著高于随机的预测能力；混合训练未能改善跨年龄泛化性能。

Conclusion: 评价理论维度在情感计算中具有理论和实践优势，尤其是跨年龄情感识别场景；多模态融合和深度学习表示对情感建模至关重要；研究提供了面向时间连续情感预测的API工具，有助于行为科学领域的情感状态测量。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into healthcare has advanced significantly, yet affect recognition remains a major challenge, particularly in AI-assisted interventions such as Computerized Cognitive Training (CCT). The THERADIA-WoZ corpus was developed to enable multimodal affect recognition in the context of AI-driven CCT, focusing on an older adult population. This study extends the corpus by introducing a dataset collected from young adults, allowing direct comparison of affect recognition models across age groups. Our objective was to assess whether multimodal models based on dimensions borrowed from appraisal theories outperform those based on categorical labels and to evaluate their generalisation power across age corpora. After comparing both corpora, models were trained and tested using within-corpus, cross-corpus, and mixed-corpus evaluation. Results revealed that appraisal dimensions consistently outperformed categorical labels across all conditions, demonstrating greater predictive accuracy and stability. Notably, categorical labels failed to generalise across age corpora, as performance dropped to chance levels in cross-corpus evaluation. In contrast, appraisal dimensions maintained predictive performance above chance, reinforcing their robustness for cross-age affect recognition. Furthermore, training on both corpora did not improve generalisation beyond within-corpus training. The findings support the theoretical and practical advantages of appraisal dimensions over categorical labels in affective computing. They also highlight the importance of multimodal fusion and deep learning representations for emotion modeling. To facilitate future research, we provide an API for researchers interested in time-continuous emotion prediction, offering valuable tools for behavioral sciences to enhance the measurement of emotional states in various experimental settings.

</details>


### [141] [MyoKin3X: A Myoelectric Framework for Full-Hand 3D Force Recording](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27949)
*Charlotte Rohleder,Raul Sîmpetru,Annika Wünsch,Alessandro Del Vecchio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MyoKin3X是一个可定制的全手框架，能同时测量最多五根手指的三维力，结合了多轴校准、手部尺寸适应和指尖专用力重建，为手部运动控制、肌肉协同和人机接口研究提供了标准化的可靠力采集方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在手指覆盖范围、力维度和解剖适应性之间存在权衡，全手可靠力采集因多轴校准、手部尺寸调整及指尖专用力重建的技术挑战而难以实现，因此需要一种同时支持所有五根手指三维力测量的标准化框架。

Method: MyoKin3X采用解剖学通用结构，集成五个三维力传感器和独立软件，支持同步肌电与力采集。提供五传感器原位交叉校准、单指/多指最大自主收缩记录，以及自动坐标变换到指尖专用坐标系。包含四种反馈模式（1D斜坡、疲劳协议、2D任意目标斜坡、2D探索任务）。

Result: 校准验证显示：轴特异性校准因子高度稳定（平均变异系数0.04%），50N时最大力误差±0.06N；轴间解耦有效（平均串扰降低92.71%，多数轴对残余串扰低于0.02%）；预测精度高（各传感器R² > 0.99）。

Conclusion: MyoKin3X实现了标准化的全手力采集，具备验证过的测量可靠性、灵活的实验协议控制和实时可视化能力，可用于高保真度的手部运动控制、肌肉协同及人机接口研究。

Abstract: Simultaneous multi-directional force measurement across all five digits is essential for studying hand coordination, compensatory forces, and myoelectric control, yet existing systems trade off digit coverage, force dimensionality, and anatomical adaptability. Reliable full-hand acquisition remains challenging because multi-axis calibration, hand-size adjustment, and consistent digit-specific force reconstruction are technically demanding. We present MyoKin3X, a customizable full-hand framework for simultaneous 3D force measurement of up to five digits providing robust and validated force reconstruction. It combines an anatomically versatile structure with five integrated 3D force sensors and a standalone software for synchronized electromyography and force acquisition. MyoKin3X provides in-place cross-calibration of all five sensors, single- and multi-digit maximal voluntary contraction recording, and automated coordinate transformation to digit-specific coordinate systems for standardized analysis across subjects and tasks. Calibration validation demonstrates high stability of the axis-specific calibration factors, with a mean coefficient of variation of 0.04% and maximum force error of +- 0.06N at 50N. It also shows effective inter-axis decoupling (mean crosstalk reduction: 92.71%; residual crosstalk below 0.02% for most axis pairs) and high predictive accuracy (R2 > 0.99 across sensors). The software includes four feedback modes: 1D ramps, fatigue protocols, 2D arbitrary target ramps, and 2D exploratory tasks. MyoKin3X therefore enables standardized full-hand force acquisition with validated measurement reliability, flexible protocol control, and real-time visualization for high-fidelity studies of hand motor control, muscle synergies, and human-machine interfacing.

</details>


### [142] [When and How AI Should Assist Brainstorming for AI Impact Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27997)
*Jarod Govers,Sanja Šćepanović,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI如何支持团队在AI影响评估中进行头脑风暴，通过设计并评估两种AI辅助的协作方法，发现AI能提升通用型AI用例的评估质量和头脑风暴体验，但在专业型用例中效果有限，并提出了AI辅助头脑风暴的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI影响评估工具未针对团队协作头脑风暴进行设计，也无法捕捉多样化团队中的不同观点，因此需要研究如何让AI有效支持团队头脑风暴过程。

Method: 从战略前瞻领域借鉴两种结构化方法，共同设计AI干预方案，在5场工作坊（28人）进行迭代设计，然后在10场工作坊（54人）中评估AI干预对头脑风暴质量和感知的影响。

Result: AI在通用型AI用例（聊天机器人伴侣）中显著提升了影响评估质量和头脑风暴感知，但在专业型用例（肾脏分配应用）中效果不明显。

Conclusion: AI在头脑风暴中应扮演支持角色：在早期构思阶段仅提供提示而非解决方案，在参与者陷入僵局或饱和时主动介入；在收敛阶段帮助结构化想法；利用专业知识优化想法；总体上应辅助流程性任务，而非替代团队珍视的构思活动本身。

Abstract: A key task in AI practice is to assess potential impacts to prevent harm. Current AI tools assisting AI impact assessment have not been designed or evaluated for collaborative team brainstorming, and they do not capture the range of views in diverse teams. We studied how AI can support team brainstorming during AI impact assessment and made three contributions. First, we adapted two structured methods from strategic foresight and co-designed AI interventions for them in five in-person workshops with 28 participants in total. Second, we evaluated the interventions in ten in-person workshops with 54 participants, finding that AI improved impact assessment quality and brainstorming perceptions for a general-purpose AI use (a chatbot companion) but not for a specialised one (a kidney allocation application). Third, our findings result in broader design guidance for AI assistance in brainstorming: AI should only offer hints and not solutions during early ideation, initiating interaction only when participants face fixation or saturation; it should facilitate structuring ideas during convergence; leverage expertise to refine ideas; and overall, it should serve more in support of tedious brainstorming process tasks, rather than ideation that teams value to do themselves.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Robot Planning and Situation Handling with Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26988)
*Austine Oloo,Zainab Altaweel,Yohei Hayamizu,Peiqi Liu,Yan Ding,Saeid Amiri,Hao Yang,Andy Kaminski,Chad Esselink,Chris Paxton,Xiaohan Zhang,Shiqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VAP-TAMP框架，通过结合动作知识与视觉语言模型，使机器人能在任务执行过程中主动感知和处理突发状况，实现长期自主运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人虽能规划复杂任务，但真实环境动态开放，执行过程中常出现门卡住、地面有障碍物等突发情况（源于自身动作失败或外部干扰），而检测和处理这些执行时突发状况仍是重大挑战，限制了机器人长期自主能力。

Method: VAP-TAMP框架利用动作知识策略性地引导视觉-语言模型进行主动视角选择与状况评估，同时构建并推理场景图，实现集成式任务与运动规划。

Result: 在仿真和移动操作平台上通过服务任务对VAP-TAMP进行了评估。

Conclusion: VAP-TAMP使机器人能够在计划执行过程中主动感知和应对突发状况。

Abstract: Current robots are capable of computing plans to accomplish complex tasks. However, real-world environments are inherently open and dynamic, and unforeseen situations frequently arise during plan execution, such as jamming doors and fallen objects on the floor. These situations may result from the robot's own action failures or from external disturbances, such as human activities. Detecting and handling such execution - time situations remains a significant challenge, limiting those robots' ability to achieve long-term autonomy. In this paper, we develop a planning and situation-handling framework, called VAP-TAMP, that enables robots to actively perceive and address unforeseen situations during plan execution. VAP-TAMP leverages action knowledge to strategically prompt vision-language models for active view selection and situation assessment, while constructing and reasoning over scene graphs for integrated task and motion planning. We evaluated VAP-TAMP using service tasks in simulation and on a mobile manipulation platform.

</details>


### [144] [Real-Time GPU-Accelerated Monte Carlo Evaluation of Safety-Critical AEB Systems Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27193)
*Akshay Karjol,Shadi Alawneh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种GPU加速的蒙特卡洛框架，用于对自动紧急制动（AEB）系统进行随机性能评估。该框架利用单线程单样本执行策略，在四款硬件平台上实现了最高54.57倍的加速比，并在Jetson AGX Orin上验证了实时可行性（530ms内运行约25,000个样本），将蒙特卡洛不确定性评估从离线验证工具转变为可部署的运行时组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AEB系统常依赖确定性的停车距离或碰撞时间阈值，未能捕捉传感、路况和车辆动力学中的不确定性。亟需一种能够在运行时进行概率性评估的框架，以满足NHTSA FMVSS No.127法规要求。

Method: 采用GPU加速的蒙特卡洛方法，基于高保真纵向车辆模型（含空气阻力、道路坡度、制动执行器动力学和重量转移效应），通过单线程单样本执行策略利用蒙特卡洛展开的独立性，同时使用确定性CPU采样保证CPU与GPU实现之间的位精确数值一致性。

Result: 在四款硬件平台（GTX 1650、RTX 5070、Jetson Orin Nano、Jetson AGX Orin）上，峰值加速比达54.57倍且数值完全一致。在完整AEB时间预算（700ms人反应时间减去120ms感知和50ms决策开销，剩余530ms）下，Jetson AGX Orin可执行约25,000个蒙特卡洛样本。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛不确定性评估可作为可部署的运行时组件而非离线验证工具，为NHTSA最终规则下的风险感知AEB阈值选择提供定量指导。

Abstract: Automatic Emergency Braking (AEB) systems represent a safety-critical national interest, with the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA) Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard (FMVSS No. 127) requiring AEB in all new light vehicles sold in the United States by September 2029. However, production implementations frequently rely on deterministic stopping-distance or Time-to-Collision (TTC) thresholds that fail to capture uncertainty in sensing, road conditions, and vehicle dynamics. This paper presents a GPU-accelerated Monte Carlo framework for stochastic evaluation of emergency braking performance using a high-fidelity longitudinal vehicle model incorporating aerodynamic drag, road grade, brake actuator dynamics, and weight transfer effects. A one-thread-per-sample execution strategy exploits the independence of Monte Carlo rollouts, while deterministic CPU-generated sampling ensures bit-exact numerical consistency between CPU and GPU implementations. The framework is evaluated across four hardware platforms spanning development and deployment environments: two laptop GPUs (GTX 1650, RTX 5070) and two automotive-grade embedded platforms (Jetson Orin Nano, Jetson AGX Orin). Peak speedups of 54.57x are achieved while maintaining exact numerical agreement. Real-time feasibility analysis with a complete AEB timing budget (700 ms human reaction time minus 120 ms perception and 50 ms decision overhead) demonstrates that the Jetson AGX Orin can execute approximately 25,000 Monte Carlo samples within a 530 ms budget, enabling real-time probabilistic AEB evaluation as part of a complete embedded pipeline. These results establish Monte Carlo-based uncertainty evaluation as a deployable runtime component rather than an offline validation tool and provide quantitative guidance for risk-aware AEB threshold selection under the NHTSA final rule.

</details>


### [145] [Interaction Forces and Internal Loads in Parallel Manipulators with Actuation Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27095)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了并联机械手中零空间力/力矩分量的适应性问题，澄清了交互力和内载荷的定义模糊，提供了均衡和操纵关节扭矩向量的显式综合方法，并通过案例研究验证了方法的有效性，纠正了文献中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献在将抓取系统中常用的交互力和内载荷概念应用于具有驱动力冗余的并联机械手时，存在关键性疏漏和定义模糊问题，需要对相关概念进行澄清和标准化。

Method: 通过分析两种最常见的零空间力/力矩分量表征方法（交互力和内载荷）在并联机械手中的适应性，识别现有文献中的疏漏，澄清定义歧义，并给出均衡和操纵关节扭矩向量的显式综合方法。

Result: 提出了针对冗余驱动并联机械手零空间力/力矩分量的清晰定义和显式计算方法，并通过案例研究验证了方法的有效性，同时纠正了文献中存在的错误结论。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了并联机械手中零空间力/力矩分量的关键概念，提供了正确的理论框架和计算方法，为后续研究和应用奠定了更可靠的基础。

Abstract: This paper discusses null-space wrench components in parallel manipulators. We examine the adaptation of the two most common characterizations of these components in grasp-like systems, namely, interaction forces and internal loads, to parallel manipulators with actuation redundancy. We identify critical oversights in the existing literature on the subject, resolve ambiguities related to the definitions of interaction forces and internal loads, and provide explicit methods for synthesizing equilibrating and manipulating joint torque vectors. A case study is also provided to justify the validity of our novel methods and correct erroneous results reported in the literature.

</details>


### [146] [An Experimental Modular Instrument With a Haptic Feedback Framework for Robotic Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27385)
*Walid Shaker,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成实时触觉反馈的模块化实验性机器人腹腔镜器械，通过腕部力/扭矩传感器估计组织交互力，在机器人手术训练系统中验证了触觉反馈相比纯视觉反馈在力调节任务中的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术虽具临床优势，但缺乏直接触觉反馈导致组织过度施力风险；现有带力反馈的商业系统成本高昂，限制了其在手术训练中的普及。

Method: 采用腕部安装的力/扭矩传感器估计工具-组织交互力，设计触觉反馈框架提取外力并渲染至触觉设备；将器械集成至RoboScope训练系统，通过力调节任务的受控用户研究进行评估。

Result: 触觉反馈相比纯视觉反馈显著提升了任务成功率、力调节精度和任务效率，实现了稳定高保真的触觉交互。

Conclusion: 该模块化器械能有效支撑机器人手术训练，解决成本与力反馈缺失的平衡问题。

Abstract: Robotic-assisted surgery offers significant clinical advantages but largely eliminates direct haptic feedback, increasing the risk of excessive tool-tissue interaction forces. Although recent commercial systems have begun to introduce force feedback, their high cost limits accessibility, particularly for surgical training. This paper presents a modular experimental robotic laparoscopic instrument integrated with a real-time haptic feedback framework. The proposed instrument employs a wrist-mounted force/torque (F/T) sensor to estimate tool-tissue interaction forces while avoiding the durability and integration challenges of tip-mounted sensors. A haptic feedback framework is developed to extract the external contact forces, render them to the haptic device, and generate stable and perceptually meaningful feedback. The instrument is integrated into the robotic surgery training system (RoboScope) and evaluated through a controlled user study involving a force regulation task. Experimental results demonstrate that haptic feedback significantly improves task success rate, force regulation accuracy, and task efficiency compared to visual-only feedback. The proposed instrument enables stable, high-fidelity haptic interaction, supporting effective robotic surgery training.

</details>


### [147] [Framework for Collaborative Operation of Autonomous Delivery Vehicles Within a Marshaling Yard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28057)
*James O'Hara,Karl Wunderlich,Gregory Stevens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去中心化动态优先级评分的编排式自主解决方案，用于封闭式调车场中电动卡车车队的全自主导航和任务分配，通过仿真验证了该方法在各种场景下均能提升车辆吞吐量并减少高需求下的设施故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭式调车场中，电动卡车需要完成充电、检查、清洁和装载等一系列顺序任务。现有的基于静态规则的孤立自主操作容易导致死锁，造成设施故障和运营中断。因此需要一种更优的协调方案来提升车辆吞吐量并避免系统瘫痪。

Method: 采用去中心化的动态优先级评分方法，根据调车场的实时状态为每个车辆计算优先级分数，从而最优地将车辆分配到不同任务中。在三种调车场规模（小、中、大）和三种需求水平（低、中、高）组合下进行仿真实验，对比静态孤立自主方案。

Result: 在所有规模与需求水平的9种组合中，编排式自主方案均显著提升了车辆吞吐量；在高需求水平下，该方案有效减少了设施故障的发生次数。

Conclusion: 将车辆低级自主性与固定基础设施相结合的编排式自主方案，能够实现封闭调车场的全自主运行，相比静态孤立自主方案具有更高的吞吐量和更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles slowly deploy into urban roads for limited use cases with significant edge case issues, closed facilities like marshaling yards provide a ripe case for combining lower-level vehicle autonomy with fixed infrastructure to create full autonomy without similar edge case concerns. Within a delivery marshaling yard, electric fleet vehicles complete a set of sequential tasks (charging, inspection, cleaning, and loading) before exiting the yard with their new load of deliveries. Hybrid automation of the vehicles and infrastructure can allow these vehicles to reach full autonomy and navigate the facility without the need of a driver, allowing for quicker movement between tasks increasing vehicle throughput. However, isolated autonomous operations based on static rules are prone to gridlock causing facility failures that temporarily shut down operations. Our orchestrated autonomy solution uses decentralized, dynamic priority scoring of vehicles based on the current status of the marshaling yard to optimally assign vehicles to tasks to increase vehicle throughput. Using a simulated facility with three marshaling yard sizes (small, medium, and large) and three demand levels (low, medium, high), we demonstrated that our orchestration solution increases vehicle throughput above static, isolated autonomy for all combinations of yard size and demand, while reducing facility failures at high demand levels.

</details>


### [148] [Flying by Inference: Active Inference World Models for Adaptive UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27935)
*Kaleem Arshid,Ali Krayani,Lucio Marcenaro,David Martin Gomez,Carlo Regazzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专家引导的主动推理框架，将多无人机编队轨迹规划从重复组合优化问题转化为分层概率推断问题：离线阶段通过遗传算法专家演示学习概率世界模型，在线阶段利用KL散度异常指标进行任务分配、路径插入、运动适应和避碰重规划，仿真和真实飞行数据验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多无人机轨迹规划依赖重复的离线组合优化，计算成本高且难以适应动态环境变化；现有强化学习方法虽可在线决策，但缺乏专家先验知识的引导，行为稳定性和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出专家引导的主动推理框架：离线阶段使用遗传算法带排斥力避碰（GA-RF）生成专家示范，构建任务、路径、运动三层字典并学习概率世界模型；在线阶段通过后验信念与专家参考分布的KL散度异常指标进行分层决策，集成EKF和PF贝叶斯估计器提升运动层轨迹修正能力。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架保留了专家级规划结构，行为比改进的Q-learning更平滑稳定；真实飞行数据验证了世界模型能在噪声和非平滑观测下修正符号预测，支持自适应无人机编队自主性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了传统组合优化和强化学习方法在适应性、计算效率和稳定性方面的不足，为无人机编队在复杂动态环境中的自主轨迹规划提供了一种可行的分层概率推断方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an expert-guided active-inference-inspired framework for adaptive UAV swarm trajectory planning. The proposed method converts multi-UAV trajectory design from a repeated combinatorial optimization problem into a hierarchical probabilistic inference problem. In the offline phase, a genetic-algorithm planner with repulsive-force collision avoidance (GA--RF) generates expert demonstrations, which are abstracted into Mission, Route, and Motion dictionaries. These dictionaries are used to learn a probabilistic world model that captures how expert mission allocations induce route orders and how route orders induce motion-level behaviors. During online operation, the UAV swarm evaluates candidate actions by forming posterior beliefs over symbolic states and minimizing KL-divergence-based abnormality indicators with respect to expert-derived reference distributions. This enables mission allocation, route insertion, motion adaptation, and collision-aware replanning without rerunning the offline optimizer. Bayesian state estimators, including EKF and PF modules, are integrated at the motion level to improve trajectory correction under uncertainty. Simulation results show that the proposed framework preserves expert-like planning structure while producing smoother and more stable behavior than modified Q-learning. Additional validation using real-flight UAV trajectory data demonstrates that the learned world model can correct symbolic predictions under noisy and non-smooth observations, supporting its applicability to adaptive UAV swarm autonomy.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Tactile-Aware Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27224)
*Pokuang Zhou,Yuhao Zhou,Quan Luu,Seungho Han,Heng Zhang,Binghao Huang,Yunzhu Li,Arash Ajoudani,Zhengtong Xu,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于四足机器人灵活操作的分层触觉感知策略学习框架，结合真实世界人类演示和仿真强化学习，使机器人能在接触丰富的场景中协调运动与操作，在真实世界任务中相比基线方法平均性能提升28.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉和本体感知无法解决接触丰富的操作任务中不确定且动态演变的交互问题，而触觉感知虽能直接观测接触信息，但目前缺乏可扩展的触觉感知学习框架用于四足机器人的运动-操作联合任务。

Method: 提出分层结构：(1) 利用真实人类演示训练触觉条件化的视觉-触觉高层策略，预测末端执行器轨迹和触觉交互线索；(2) 在仿真中进行大规模强化学习，训练触觉感知的全身体控制策略，实现零样本迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在手内重定向与插入、阀门拧紧、精细物体操作等真实世界任务中，相比仅视觉和视觉-触觉基线方法，平均性能提升28.54%。

Conclusion: 所提出的触觉感知分层学习框架能有效实现四足机器人在接触丰富场景下的运动与操作协调，且具备良好的真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal loco-manipulation is commonly built on visual perception and proprioception. Yet reliable contact-rich manipulation remains difficult: vision and proprioception alone cannot resolve uncertain, evolving interactions with the environment. Tactile sensing offers direct contact observability, but scalable tactile-aware learning framework for quadrupedal loco-manipulation is still underexplored. In this paper, we present a tactile-aware loco-manipulation policy learning pipeline with a hierarchical structure. Our approach has two key components. First, we leverage real-world human demonstrations to train a tactile-conditioned visuotactile high-level policy. This policy predicts not only end-effector trajectories for manipulation, but also the evolving tactile interaction cues that characterize how contact should develop over time. Second, we perform large-scale reinforcement learning in simulation to learn a tactile-aware whole-body control policy that tracks diverse commanded trajectories and tactile interaction cues, and transfers zero-shot to the real world. Together, these components enable coordinated locomotion and manipulation under contact-rich scenarios. We evaluate the system on real-world contact-rich tasks, including in-hand reorientation with insertion, valve tightening, and delicate object manipulation. Compared to vision-only and visuotactile baselines, our method improves performance by 28.54% on average across these tasks.

</details>


### [150] [DOT-Sim: Differentiable Optical Tactile Simulation with Precise Real-to-Sim Physical Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27367)
*Yang You,Won Kyung Do,Aiden Swann,Rika Antonova,Monroe Kennedy,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DOT-Sim提出了一种基于可微分的材料点法(MPM)的光学触觉传感器仿真框架，能够准确模拟软体传感器的物理形变与光学响应，并支持快速标定（数分钟内），在零样本仿真到现实任务中实现了高精度分类（85%-90%）和低误差轨迹跟踪（<0.9mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学触觉传感器仿真面临两大挑战：软体传感器高形变与复杂光学特性的物理建模困难。先前的工作使用简化模型导致仿真不准确，且标定过程耗时。本文旨在实现物理精确且高效的仿真，以弥合仿真与现实之间的差距。

Method: 采用材料点法(MPM)将软体传感器建模为弹性材料以精确捕获物理行为；通过少量真实数据在数分钟内完成快速标定；提出学习残差图像的方法模拟光学响应，即从真实世界的空闲状态图像学习偏移量；支持大规模非线性形变。

Result: 在DenseTact传感器上实现物理动力学精确复现；接触丰富场景下生成逼真光学输出；零样本仿真到现实部署中，分类准确率达85%（挑战性物体）和90%（嵌入式肿瘤检测）；轨迹跟踪平均误差小于0.9mm。

Conclusion: DOT-Sim通过MPM物理建模与残差学习光学模拟，实现了光学触觉传感器的高保真仿真与快速标定，在零样本迁移任务中展示了优异的仿真到现实性能，为触觉传感器的仿真应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Simulating optical tactile sensors presents significant challenges due to their high deformability and intricate optical properties. To address these issues and enable a physically accurate simulation, we propose DOT-Sim: Differentiable Optical Tactile Simulation. Unlike prior simulators that rely on simplified models of deformable sensors, DOT-Sim accurately captures the physical behavior of soft sensors by modeling them as elastic materials using the Material Point Method (MPM). DOT-Sim enables rapid calibration of optical tactile sensor simulation using a small number of demonstrations within minutes, which is substantially faster than existing methods. Compared to current baselines, our approach supports much larger and non-linear deformations. To handle the optical aspect, we propose a novel approach to simulating optical responses by learning a residual image relative to the real-world idle state. We validate the physical and visual realism of our method through a series of zero-shot sim-to-real tasks. Our experiments show that DOT-Sim (1) accurately replicates the physical dynamics of a DenseTact optical tactile sensor in reality, (2) generates realistic optical outputs in contact-rich scenarios, (3) enables direct deployment of simulation-trained classifiers in the real world, achieving 85% classification accuracy on challenging objects and 90% accuracy in embedded tumor-type detection, and (4) allows precise trajectory following with a policy trained from demonstrations in simulation, with an average error of less than 0.9 mm.

</details>


### [151] [RAY-TOLD: Ray-Based Latent Dynamics for Dense Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance with TDMPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27450)
*Seungho Han,Seokju Lee,Jeonguk Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAY-TOLD混合控制架构，通过将激光雷达数据编码为潜在状态表征，结合MPPI的短视鲁棒控制与强化学习的长视前瞻能力，并引入策略混合采样策略，在密集动态人群环境中有效降低碰撞率，提升导航可靠性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集动态人群环境对自主移动机器人构成持续挑战，纯反应式规划方法（如MPPI）因预测视野有限，易陷入复杂场景中的局部最优。

Method: 提出RAY-TOLD混合控制架构，包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于激光雷达的潜在动力学模型，将高维传感器数据编码为紧凑状态表征；(2) 学习终端值函数与策略先验；(3) 策略混合采样策略，用学习到的策略生成轨迹增强MPPI候选种群。

Result: 在密集动态障碍物的随机环境中进行广泛测试，与MPPI基线相比，该方法显著降低了碰撞率。

Conclusion: 将短视的基于物理的轨迹展开与学习到的长视意图相结合，能够显著提升导航的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Dense, dynamic crowds pose a persistent challenge for autonomous mobile robots. Purely reactive planning methods, such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control, often fail to escape local minima in complex scenarios due to their limited prediction horizon. To bridge this gap, we propose Ray-based Task-Oriented Latent Dynamics (RAY-TOLD), a hybrid control architecture that integrates obstacle information into latent dynamics and utilizes the robustness of physics-based MPPI with the long-horizon foresight of reinforcement learning. RAY-TOLD leverages a LiDAR-centric latent dynamics model to encode high-dimensional sensor data into a compact state representation, enabling the learning of a terminal value function and a policy prior. We introduce a policy mixture sampling strategy that augments the MPPI candidate population with trajectories derived from the learned policy, effectively guiding the planner towards the goal while maintaining kinematic feasibility. Extensive tests in a stochastic environment with high-density dynamic obstacles demonstrate that our method outperforms the MPPI baseline, reducing the collision rate. The results confirm that blending short-horizon physics-based rollouts with learned long-horizon intent significantly enhances navigation reliability and safety.

</details>


### [152] [Function-based Parametric Co-Design Optimization of Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27557)
*Mohammad Amin Mirzaee,Harsh Gupta,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的参数化框架，用于机器人手部生成，将手掌结构、手指运动学、指尖几何和表面曲率整合到单一设计空间中，通过参数化表面变形影响接触交互，并在抓取稳定性任务中验证了设计优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人手部设计与任务驱动评估和控制脱节，缺乏系统性优化；已有的协同设计方法范围有限，仅优化少数设计参数。

Method: 提出了一个全面的参数化框架，统一了手掌结构、手指运动学、指尖几何和精细表面曲率；通过参数化表面变形核引入精细几何特征以影响接触交互。

Result: 该框架在仿真和真实动态场景的抓取稳定性任务中验证有效，能生成可直接用于仿真和制造的完整手部模型。

Conclusion: 该框架将开源，以加速灵巧手协同设计优化及跨具身策略训练与控制研究。

Abstract: Despite advances in dexterous hand manipulation, robotic hand design is still largely decoupled from task-driven evaluation and control, limiting systematic optimization. Existing robotic hand co-design approaches are often limited in scope, optimizing a small subset of design parameters. We introduce a comprehensive parametric framework for robotic hand generation that unifies palm structure, finger kinematics, fingertip geometry, and fine-scale surface curvatures within a single design space. Fine geometric features are introduced through parametric surface deformation kernels that directly influence contact interactions. We validate the framework on design optimization in grasp stability tasks in simulation and real-world dynamic scenarios. Our framework produces simulation- and fabrication-ready hand models and will be released as open-source to enable rapid design iteration for dexterous hand co-design optimization frameworks and cross-embodiment policy training and control research.

</details>


### [153] [Robot Learning from Human Videos: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27621)
*Junyi Ma,Erhang Zhang,Haoran Yang,Ditao Li,Chenyang Xu,Guangming Wang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了从人类视频中学习机器人操作技能的方法，重点关注人-机器人技能迁移和数据基础，提出了任务、观测、动作三个层次的知识迁移分类体系，并分析了不同数据配置与学习范式的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域面临的数据扩展瓶颈阻碍了具身AI和机器人技术的进一步发展，而人类活动视频数据丰富且易于获取，利用这些数据实现机器人技能学习被认为是规模化训练通用机器人系统的可行路径。

Method: 首先回顾机器人策略学习基础，描述融合人类视频的基本接口；然后提出层次化分类体系，将人类视频到机器人技能的迁移分为任务导向、观测导向和动作导向三类通路，并分析了它们与不同数据配置和学习范式的跨族耦合关系；最后调研了常用人类视频数据集和视频生成方案。

Result: 形成了人类视频驱动的机器人技能学习综述，建立了迁移路径的分类框架，提供了数据集开发和利用的大规模统计趋势。

Conclusion: 该领域仍面临诸多挑战和局限性，未来研究需要在多个方向上进一步探索。

Abstract: A critical bottleneck hindering further advancement in embodied AI and robotics is the challenge of scaling robot data. To address this, the field of learning robot manipulation skills from human video data has attracted rapidly growing attention in recent years, driven by the abundance of human activity videos and advances in computer vision. This line of research promises to enable robots to acquire skills passively from the vast and readily available resource of human demonstrations, substantially favoring scalable learning for generalist robotic systems. Therefore, we present this survey to provide a comprehensive and up-to-date review of human-video-based learning techniques in robotics, focusing on both human-robot skill transfer and data foundations. We first review the policy learning foundations in robotics, and then describe the fundamental interfaces to incorporate human videos. Subsequently, we introduce a hierarchical taxonomy of transferring human videos to robot skills, covering task-, observation-, and action-oriented pathways, along with a cross-family analysis of their couplings with different data configurations and learning paradigms. In addition, we investigate the data foundations including widely-used human video datasets and video generation schemes, and provide large-scale statistical trends in dataset development and utilization. Ultimately, we emphasize the challenges and limitations intrinsic to this field, and delineate potential avenues for future research. The paper list of our survey is available at https://github.com/IRMVLab/awesome-robot-learning-from-human-videos.

</details>


### [154] [ExoActor: Exocentric Video Generation as Generalizable Interactive Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27711)
*Yanghao Zhou,Jingyu Ma,Yibo Peng,Zhenguo Sun,Yu Bai,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoActor利用大规模视频生成模型的泛化能力，通过第三人称视频生成作为统一接口建模人形机器人与环境、物体的交互行为，再将合成视频转化为可执行的人形动作序列，实现零样本泛化到新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制系统中，建模机器人、环境与任务相关物体之间的流畅交互行为仍然是一个基础性挑战，因为需要联合捕捉空间上下文、时间动态、机器人动作和任务意图，而这与传统的监督学习方式不匹配。

Method: 提出ExoActor框架，利用大规模视频生成模型的泛化能力，将第三人称视频生成作为统一接口建模交互动态。先根据任务指令和场景上下文合成执行过程视频，再通过人体运动估计和通用运动控制器将其转化为可执行的人形行为序列。

Result: 实现了一个端到端系统，验证了该框架能够泛化到新场景，无需额外的真实世界数据采集。

Conclusion: ExoActor提供了一种可扩展的建模丰富交互行为的方法，为生成模型推动通用人形智能开辟了新途径，同时讨论了当前实现的局限性和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Humanoid control systems have made significant progress in recent years, yet modeling fluent interaction-rich behavior between a robot, its surrounding environment, and task-relevant objects remains a fundamental challenge. This difficulty arises from the need to jointly capture spatial context, temporal dynamics, robot actions, and task intent at scale, which is a poor match to conventional supervision. We propose ExoActor, a novel framework that leverages the generalization capabilities of large-scale video generation models to address this problem. The key insight in ExoActor is to use third-person video generation as a unified interface for modeling interaction dynamics. Given a task instruction and scene context, ExoActor synthesizes plausible execution processes that implicitly encode coordinated interactions between robot, environment, and objects. Such video output is then transformed into executable humanoid behaviors through a pipeline that estimates human motion and executes it via a general motion controller, yielding a task-conditioned behavior sequence. To validate the proposed framework, we implement it as an end-to-end system and demonstrate its generalization to new scenarios without additional real-world data collection. Furthermore, we conclude by discussing limitations of the current implementation and outlining promising directions for future research, illustrating how ExoActor provides a scalable approach to modeling interaction-rich humanoid behaviors, potentially opening a new avenue for generative models to advance general-purpose humanoid intelligence.

</details>


### [155] [Connected Dependability Cage: Run-Time Function and Anomaly Monitoring for the Development and Operation of Safe Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27728)
*Iqra Aslam,Nour Habib,Abhishek Buragohain,Meng Zhang,Andreas Rausch,Vaibhav Tiwari,Mohamed Benchat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Connected Dependability Cage”的架构框架，通过集成的功能监控器和异常监控器，实现AI感知系统的分层故障安全操作，确保自动驾驶在动态不可预测环境中的安全合规性，并支持最小风险策略与持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在动态不可预测环境中面临复杂安全挑战，AI感知系统必须可靠运行；现有的安全标准（如ISO 26262、ISO/PASSOTIF）主要关注功能安全，但高等级自动驾驶需要故障运行能力，以应对系统故障和未知场景，现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 提出Connected Dependability Cage架构，集成两种互补监控机制：1) 功能监控器，通过投票机制监督多条异构AI感知管线并检测不一致性；2) 异常监控器，通过检测训练数据集中未包含的未知或新异物体来评估AI感知可靠性。在检测到关键差异时，系统执行优雅降级，最终过渡到最小风险策略。任一监控器触发安全标志时，自动启动数据记录以支持迭代开发。

Result: 两种监控器均已完成实现，并通过大量实车测试验证了其在真实应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: Connected Dependability Cage框架能够有效实现AI感知系统的分层故障安全行为，满足自动驾驶安全需求，并通过自动数据记录支持持续的系统改进。

Abstract: The advancement of automated vehicles introduces complex safety challenges, particularly in dynamic and unpredictable environments where AI-enabled perception systems must operate reliably. Ensuring compliance with safety standards such as ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF) is essential for addressing system malfunctions and mitigating unsafe behavior in unknown scenarios. However, as automation levels increase, vehicles must go beyond conventional functional safety by incorporating fail-operational capabilities that enable continued safe operation during system or component failures and the handling of unfamiliar or degraded operational conditions. To address these safety concerns, we propose the Connected Dependability Cage, an architectural framework designed to enable hierarchical fail-operational behavior in AI-enabled perception systems. This framework integrates two complementary monitoring mechanisms: a Function Monitor that oversees multiple heterogeneous AI-based perception pipelines and detects inconsistencies through a voting mechanism, and an Anomaly Monitor that evaluates the reliability of AI perception by detecting unknown or novel objects in scenes that may be excluded from the training dataset. In the presence of critical discrepancies, the system supports graceful degradation, ultimately enabling a transition to a minimal-risk maneuver strategy. Furthermore, whenever either monitor raises a safety flag, an automated data recording process is initiated to facilitate iterative system development and continuous improvement. Both monitors have been implemented and validated through extensive vehicle testing, demonstrating their practical effectiveness in real-world applications.

</details>


### [156] [MotuBrain: An Advanced World Action Model for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27792)
*MotuBrain Team,Chendong Xiang,Fan Bao,Haitian Liu,Hengkai Tan,Hongzhe Bi,James Li,Jiabao Liu,Jingrui Pang,Kiro Jing,Louis Liu,Mengchen Cai,Rongxu Cui,Ruowen Zhao,Runqing Wang,Shuhe Huang,Yao Feng,Yinze Rong,Zeyuan Wang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO
